    As our bestselling product category introduced in 2023, plush products                    毛絨產品作為我們在2023 年推出的爆款產品品
    achieved a revenue of RMB446.1 million in the first half of 2024, accounting              類，於2024年上半年，實現收入人民幣446.1百
    for 9.8% of our revenue. During this period, we continued to explore the                  萬元，佔比達到9.8%。在此期間，我們持續深
    cores of IPs and innovate our products, such as rotocast plush toys. The                  挖 IP 內核，並不斷進行產品創新，例如搪膠毛
    rotocasting process makes the facial expressions more vivid and attractive,               絨類玩具。搪膠工藝使得面部表情更為鮮活靈
    and the plush toys inherently allow easy display and can serve as stylish                 動、可愛迷人，而毛絨玩具又具備方便展示、
    outfit accessories. Such combination not only enhances the emotional bond                 能作為穿搭配飾等特性，這不僅增強了粉絲與IP
    between fans and IPs, but also provides a richer and more diverse interactive             的情感紐帶，還提供了更為豐富多樣的互動體
    experiences. While attracting many new fans, it also greatly enhances the                 驗，在吸引眾多新粉絲的同時，也極大地增強
    stickiness of fans to IPs.                                                                了粉絲對IP的黏性。


    In mid-June 2024, we launched our first building block product. Through                   2024年6月中旬，我們上架了首款積木產品。該
    block stacking, the product shows the spiritual core of our IPs. It not only              產品借助積木拼搭的形式，展現了 IP 的精神內
    enhances the ways in which the IPs are represented but also broadens the                  核，不僅豐富了 IP 的呈現方式，還拓展了粉絲
    channels for fan interaction with IPs, enabling fans to explore the deeper                與IP互動體驗的渠道，使粉絲能夠更深入的了解
    narratives that underlie our IPs. On the basis of compatibility with the                  我們IP背後的故事。在兼容傳統積木體系的基礎

     the results of the Group. Investors shall not treat non-IFRS financial indicator     者不應認為非國際財務報告準則的財務指標替
     as an alternative or better version of the results of the Group prepared in          代或由於本集團的國際財務報告準則會計準則
     accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards. In addition, not all companies            的業績。此外，可能不是所有公司會以相同

Foreign Exchange Risk Management                                                    外匯風險管理
As the Company’s subsidiaries operate in Mainland China, Hong Kong, Macao,          本公司的附屬公司在中國內地、港澳台及海外
Taiwan and overseas, they are exposed to foreign exchange risk arising from         經營，面對若干貨幣敞口（主要有關美元、泰銖
certain currency exposure (mainly related to US dollar, Thai Baht and Hong          及港幣）引致的外匯風險。由於本集團以我們經
Kong dollar). Our management considers that the business is not exposed to          營實體各自功能貨幣以外貨幣計值的重大金融
any significant foreign exchange risk as the financial assets and liabilities of    資產及負債不重大，故管理層認為業務並無任
our Group denominated in currencies other than the respective functional            何重大外匯風險敞口。本集團並沒有對沖外幣

on relevant developments and take measures when it is necessary to ensure

     (a) Purpose of the Scheme                                                          (a) 計劃的目的
         The purpose of the Post-IPO Share Award Scheme are: (i) to align the             首次公開發售後股份獎勵計劃的目的為：

         of Shares, dividends and other distributions paid on Shares and/or the           他分派及 ╱ 或提升股份價值令合資格人士
         increase in value of the Shares; and (ii) to encourage and retain eligible       的利益與本集團的利益一致；及(ii)鼓勵並
         persons to make contributions to the long-term growth and profits of             挽留合資格人士，以就本集團的長期增長
         the Group.                                                                       及溢利作出貢獻。

The Audit Committee comprises two independent non-executive Directors          審核委員會由兩名獨立非執行董事和一名非執
and one non-executive Director, namely, Mr. Wu Liansheng, Mr. Ngan King        行董事組成，分別為吳聯生先生、顏勁良先生
Leung Gary and Mr. Tu Zheng. The chairman of the Audit Committee is            及屠錚先生。審核委員會主席為吳聯生先生，
Mr. Wu Liansheng, and Mr. Ngan King Leung Gary, a member of the Audit          審核委員會成員顏勁良先生具專業會計資格。
Committee, has a professional qualification in accountancy.


The Audit Committee has reviewed with management the accounting                審核委員會已與管理層審閱本集團採納之會計
principles and practices adopted by the Group, and discussed internal          準則及慣例，並討論內部監控及財務匯報事
controls and financial reporting matters, including a review of the interim    宜，包括審閱截至2024年6月30日止六個月之中
financial information for the six months ended 30 June 2024.                   期財務資料。


The Company’s external auditor, PricewaterhouseCoopers, has performed          本公司外聘核數師羅兵咸永道會計師事務所已
a review of the Group’s interim financial information for the six months       根據國際審閱委聘準則第2410號「由實體的獨立
ended 30 June 2024 in accordance with the International Standard on            核數師執行中期財務資料審閱」，對本集團截至
Review Engagements 2410, “Review of Interim Financial Information              2024年6月30日止六個月的中期財務資料進行審
Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity”. Based on their            閱。羅兵咸永道會計師事務所已確認，按照他
review, PricewaterhouseCoopers confirmed that nothing has come to their        們的審閱，並無發現任何事項致令他們相信中
attention that causes them to believe that the interim financial information   期財務資料在各重大方面未有根據國際會計準
is not prepared, in all material respects, in accordance with International    則第34號「中期財務報告」編製。
Accounting Standard 34 “Interim Financial Reporting”.

      mainly from exposure of trade receivables and other receivables                      的信用風險主要來自於從事經營活動

      “Financial Instrument”.                                                              虧損」模型。

  4.4 Fair value estimation (Continued)                                               4.4 公平值估計（續）
      (i) Fair value hierarchy (Continued)                                                (i) 公平值層級（續）
          The Group analyses the financial instruments carried at fair                        本集團採用估值方法分析以公平值
          value by valuation method. The different levels have been                           列賬的金融工具。不同層級的定義
          defined as follow:                                                                  如下：


          Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in active                   層級1: 在活躍市場買賣的金融工具
          markets (such as publicly traded derivatives, and equity                           （如公開交易之衍生工具及股本證
          securities) is based on quoted market prices at the end of the                      券）之公平值乃基於報告期末的市
          reporting period. The quoted market price used for financial                        場報價。本集團持有的金融資產使
          assets held by the Group is the current bid price. These                            用的市場報價為當前買入價。該等
          instruments are included in Level 1.                                                工具列入層級1中。


          Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that are not                       層級2: 並非於活躍市場買賣的金融
          traded in an active market (for example, over-the-counter                           工具（如場外衍生工具）之公平值
          derivatives) is determined using valuation techniques which                         乃採用估值技術釐定，該等估值技
          maximise the use of observable market data and rely as                              術盡量使用可觀察市場數據而極少
          little as possible on entity-specific estimates. If all significant                 依賴於實體的特定估計。倘計算工
          inputs required to fair value an instrument are observable, the                     具之公平值所需的全部重大輸入數
          instrument is included in Level 2.                                                  據均為可觀察數據，則該工具列入
                                                                                              層級2中。


          Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on                   層級3: 如一項或多項重大輸入數據
          observable market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                      並非根據可觀察市場數據得出，則
          This is the case for investment in associates measured at fair                      該工具列入層級3 中。按公平值計
          value through profit or loss, other unlisted investment and                         量且其變動計入損益的聯營公司投
          private equity fund investments. Further details of the Group’s                     資、其他非上市投資及私募股權基
          financial assets that are measure at fair value using Level 3                       金投資屬該等情況。有關本集團使
          inputs are given in Note 4.4(ii) and Note 4.4(iii) below.                           用層級3 輸入值以公平值計量的金
                                                                                              融資產的進一步詳情載列於下文附
                                                                                              註4.4(ii)及附註4.4(iii)。

     21 SHARE-BASED COMPENSATION                                                    21 以股份為基礎的報酬
        The Company adopted the restricted share unit (the “RSU”) long term          本公司於2020年7月24日採納受限制股份單
        incentive plan (the “Post-IPO Share Award Scheme”) on 24 July 2020 and       位（「受限制股份單位」）長期激勵計劃（「首
        took effective from the Listing date and amended on 17 May 2023. As          次公開發售後股份獎勵計劃」），自上市日
        at 30 June 2024, the remaining life of the Post-IPO Share Award Scheme       期起生效，並於 2023 年 5 月 17 日修訂。於
        was approximately six years and six months. The objective of the Post-       2024年6月30日，首次公開發售後股份獎勵
        IPO Share Award Scheme is to align the interests of selected participants    計劃的剩餘期限約為六年零六個月。首次
        with those of the Group and to encourage and retain selected                 公開發售後股份獎勵計劃之目的乃使選定
        participants to make contributions to the long-term growth and profits       參與者的利益與本集團利益保持一致，鼓
        of the Group.                                                                勵及挽留選定參與者為本集團長遠增長及
                                                                                     溢利作出貢獻。


        Under the Post-IPO Share Award Scheme, Pop Mart Partner Limited (the         根據首次公開發售後股份獎勵計劃，P o p
        “Share Award Trust”) was established in British Virgin Islands on 7 May      Mart Partner Limited（「股份獎勵信託」）於
        2020. As the financial and operational policies of the Share Award Trust     2020 年5 月7 日在英屬維京群島成立。由於
        are governed by the Group, and the Group benefits from the Share             股份獎勵信託的財務及運營政策受本集團
        Award Trust’s activities, the Share Award Trust is consolidated in the       管理，且本集團從股份獎勵信託的活動中
        Group’s financial statements as a special purpose entity.                    受益，故股份獎勵信託於本集團財務報表

  Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly         若一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
  or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence            在作出財務及經營決策時對另一方發揮重
  over the other party in making financial and operation decisions.                   大影響，即視為有關聯。倘所涉各方受共
  Parties are also considered to be related if they are subject to common             同控制，則亦視為有關聯。泡泡瑪特的主
  control. Members of key management and their close family member of                 要管理層成員及其近親成員亦被視為關聯
  Pop Mart are also considered as related parties.                                    方。


  Save as disclosed elsewhere in this interim condensed consolidated                  除本中期簡明綜合財務資料其他章節所披
  financial information, the Group has the following significant related              露者外，本集團亦有以下重大關聯交易，
  transactions which were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s           該等交易乃於本集團日常業務過程中進
  business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.                        行，並按共同商定的條款釐定。


  All the transaction with related parties were conducted in accordance               與關聯方的所有交易均按照本集團與關聯
  with the terms of contracts entered into between the Group and the                  方訂立的合約條款進行。
  related parties.

      「服務提供者分項限額」                      根據本公司採納的所有股份計劃項下的購股權及 ╱ 或本公司新股份獎勵的計劃限額而授
